Finance Tracker runtime dependency notice collection

Existing upstream notices are reproduced below. Component licenses remain separate
from the owner-authored project grant. Metadata declarations are identified as such;
no absent copyright year or holder is invented. Exact versions/source locations are
in package-lock.json and docs/licensing/DEPENDENCY_LICENSES.json.
